884 entries, filing-verifier to fire-circle-spec-writer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

It re-establishes from evidence that the tests actually pass, the review actually happened, the acceptance criteria are actually met and the change actually runs, reproducing the proof rather than reading a claim of it. Use as the final gate on any revision before it ships or is handed back as complete. - **finding-investigator**: Turn a candidate into a traceable investigation plan. Record expected behavior, actual behavior, evidence, reproduction steps and false-positive analysis. ([Sievehouse/shap-review-toolkit](https://github.com/Sievehouse/shap-review-toolkit/blob/HEAD/agents/finding-investigator.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **finding-investigator**: Investigates whether an observed phenomenon is a real finding (signal) or noise (artifact, n-too-small, methodological confound). Use when something unexpected shows up in a run and you want a careful read before recording it in trellis-docs/trellis-findings.md. Input: a description of the phenomenon, plus the run(s) where it was observed. Output: a verdict (SIGNAL / NOISE / NEED_MORE_DATA) with evidence and a draft trellis-docs/trellis-findings.md entry if SIGNAL. ([Mike-Byrne-AI/specdec-sidechannels](https://github.com/Mike-Byrne-AI/specdec-sidechannels/blob/HEAD/lockstep/.claude/agents/finding-investigator.md) · ★1)
- **finding-judge**: Post-reviewer finding judge — one role, two substrates (SPEC §4.13). Called by `/review` (step 3.5), `/ship` (step 1.5), and `/activate` (step 3.5) after a reviewer produces findings and BEFORE the author acts on them, to rule each finding confirmed/refuted with the command it ran, assign an action, and emit a durable, anti-swing-checked fix list. It judges findings; it never authors the fix. It emits no `ship`/`approve` token — it is not a vote and not a merge gate. ([ilgyu-yi/ghjig-claude](https://github.com/ilgyu-yi/ghjig-claude/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/finding-judge.md) · ★2)
- **finding-judge**: nightshift's Tier 2 for the reviewer. Decides what happens to each review finding (fix, drop, hold, out of scope, standards) from a bounded packet, and adds what the reviewers missed. Started by the review-prs skill with the paths of review.md, diff.patch and issue.md. ([ChrispyCode21/nightshift](https://github.com/ChrispyCode21/nightshift/blob/HEAD/agents/finding-judge.md))
- **finding-linker**: Compares new findings against a shortlist of existing findings in the vault and proposes typed edges (supports / contradicts / extends / uses / similar-to). Invoked by /paperloom:ingest. ([trapoom555/claude-paperloom](https://github.com/trapoom555/claude-paperloom/blob/HEAD/agents/finding-linker.md) · ★95)
- **finding-linker**: Compares new findings against a shortlist of existing wiki findings and proposes typed edges (supports / contradicts / extends / uses / similar-to). Invoked once per /paper-ingest. Returns JSON only; `lib apply-edges` writes and mirrors them. ([marvinsxtr/claude-librarian](https://github.com/marvinsxtr/claude-librarian/blob/HEAD/claude_librarian/agents/finding-linker.md) · ★3)
- **finding-merger**: Mechanical — groups review findings that describe the same defect, so each defect is verified and fixed once ([zb-ss/claude-plugin-workflow](https://github.com/zb-ss/claude-plugin-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/finding-merger.md) · ★1)
- **finding-merger**: Owns Phase 5 — ingests every discovery artifact across all rounds, clusters findings by root cause rather than symptom, runs a falsification pass that re-reads the cited code to attempt disproof, and emits audit-output/05-findings-triaged.md. Use after Phase 4 discovery completes, or standalone to merge and falsify a pile of raw findings from multiple sources. ([calc1f4r/Horus](https://github.com/calc1f4r/Horus/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/finding-merger.md) · 2 copies)
- **finding-prover**: Refute gate for one normalized review finding: tries to explain the finding away from the source and, failing that, proposes one cheap check that would settle it. Holds Read, Grep and Glob only, so it runs nothing. Dispatched by the proof stage of the review plugin's findings normalization (/review:fanout, /review:council); not intended for direct ad-hoc use. ([melodic-software/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/melodic-software/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/review/agents/finding-prover.md) · ★32)
- **finding-quality-auditor**: Audits finding quality and removes low-quality findings. ([vstakhovsky/shokunin-review](https://github.com/vstakhovsky/shokunin-review/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/finding-quality-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **finding-refuter**: Adversarially tries to refute a single code-review finding and returns SURVIVES, REFUTED, or PRE-EXISTING with the evidence behind each of the six refutation questions. The caller decides when to dispatch it; the review that invoked it runs the same pass itself by default. ([AlexJSully/AlexJSully-Portfolio](https://github.com/AlexJSully/AlexJSully-Portfolio/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/audit-pr/agents/finding-refuter.md) · ★3)
- **finding-refuter**: Phase 5 adversarial refuter, judges reviewer findings BEFORE the address-all loop spends a fix on them. Takes every finding of a review round verbatim and returns one verdict each, UPHELD (claim survives, cite the reachable entry point or the real rule), REFUTED (cite the file:line guard, carve-out, or missing catalog ID that makes it wrong, plus what would change your mind), or ESCALATED (real and worse than stated). Defaults to KEEPING the finding, uncertainty is never a refutation, because dropping a real defect costs more than fixing a phantom. Dispatch exactly ONE refuter per review roun… ([nadyshalaby/hackify](https://github.com/nadyshalaby/hackify/blob/HEAD/agents/finding-refuter.md) · ★3)
- **finding-refuter**: Skeptic who tries to REFUTE one code-review finding by constructing a concrete failing scenario from the actual code at a given sha. Returns real=true only with a reproduction path; defaults to real=false when uncertain. Used to filter findings before a developer is asked to fix them. ([shishir10/claude-sous](https://github.com/shishir10/claude-sous/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/finding-refuter.md) · ★3)
- **finding-refuter**: Adversarial reader whose job is to KILL a proposed QA finding before it is filed. Burden of proof reversed — it defaults to REFUTED when uncertain and must be forced to concede. Checks the six ways a finding is usually wrong here — harness artifact, working as designed, evidence does not show what it claims, wrong causal chain, unreachable state, already known. Use from /ct-qa (one agent per candidate finding, in parallel) before anything reaches FINDINGS.md. Returns a verdict; never edits and never files. Advisory tier — non-blocking. ([CT2689-Tech/DeclutrMail](https://github.com/CT2689-Tech/DeclutrMail/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/finding-refuter.md))
- **finding-refuter**: Adversarially verifies ONE review finding by trying to REFUTE it against the actual code — reads the implicated files, dependency sources, and configs, and returns a verdict with line-level evidence. Defaults to not-real when the failure mechanism cannot be confirmed. Used by adversarial-diff-review as the gate between "plausible finding" and "fix it". ([Drozdetskiy/claude-skills](https://github.com/Drozdetskiy/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/finding-refuter.md))
- **finding-reporter**: Phase 14 per-finding report authoring agent. Reads a single finding directory (draft.md, debate.md, adversarial-review.md, poc script, evidence/) and writes the disclosure-ready report.md via the vuln-report skill. Runs cold-context per finding so the heavyweight PoC-building workload cannot starve the report-writing step. ([vigolium/piolium](https://github.com/vigolium/piolium/blob/HEAD/agents/finding-reporter.md) · ★152)
- **finding-responder**: Author-side reception of code-review findings for `/review-respond`. Re-verifies each finding against current codebase reality (not the reviewer's word) and classifies it agree / disagree (reasoned) / needs-human — never performative agreement. Under --fix only, applies agreed findings one at a time via the sanctioned lib/respond.mjs apply path. Advisory by default. ([Tahmid12Khan/agentic-workflows](https://github.com/Tahmid12Khan/agentic-workflows/blob/HEAD/agents/finding-responder.md))
- **finding-reviewer**: Reviews changes to checks, the engine, or the AI layer for review-safety and contract violations. Use after adding or changing a check, before committing. ([deepak-terse/agentic-coding](https://github.com/deepak-terse/agentic-coding/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/finding-reviewer.md))
- **finding-reviewer**: Independently reviews findings reported by Logbook's review agents (bug-hunter, security-scanner, import-tester and the rest) and decides, for each one, whether it is a real issue to record in docs/phases/known-issues.md, a duplicate, not a bug, a choice that needs an open question, or something the reporting agent must investigate further — in which case it writes the exact questions to send back. Used by the issue-sweep skill; also use it directly when asked "is this a real bug", "check this finding" or "should this go in known issues". Re-runs proofs; never edits files. ([gwpreston16/Logbook](https://github.com/gwpreston16/Logbook/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/finding-reviewer.md))
- **finding-reviewer**: Independently reviews findings reported by Renovo's review agents (bug-catcher, security-scanner, performance-auditor and the rest) and decides, for each one, whether it is a real issue to record in docs/phases/known-issues.md, a duplicate, not a bug, a choice that needs a decision first, or something the reporting agent must investigate further — in which case it writes the exact questions to send back. Used by the issue-sweep skill; also use it directly when asked "is this a real bug", "check this finding" or "should this go in known issues". Re-runs proofs; never edits files. ([gwpreston16/Renovo](https://github.com/gwpreston16/Renovo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/finding-reviewer.md))
- **finding-sharing**: During parallel flux-drive reviews, agents can share high-severity findings via `{OUTPUT_DIR}/peer-findings.jsonl`. ([mistakeknot/interflux](https://github.com/mistakeknot/interflux/blob/HEAD/agents/finding-sharing.md) · ★1)
- **finding-skeptic**: Adversarial skeptic that tries to REFUTE a single recorded review finding against the unit diff; returns UPHELD / REFUTED-not-real / REFUTED-over-rated. Read-only. ([SyloRei/claude-godmode](https://github.com/SyloRei/claude-godmode/blob/HEAD/agents/finding-skeptic.md) · ★3)
- **finding-skeptic**: Use to try to refute one review finding before it is reported, one agent per finding per lens, dispatched by the review playbooks. Reproduces the claim, looks for the reading under which the code is correct, checks the finding is this change's doing, checks the cited rule exists, and checks the fix. Read-only. Defaults to refuted when it cannot confirm. ([DariuszDusza/agentic-workflow-foundry](https://github.com/DariuszDusza/agentic-workflow-foundry/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/finding-skeptic.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **finding-skeptic**: Adversarially verifies ONE code-review finding by trying to refute it against the actual code. Dispatched by the jira-tasks auto-review gate, one skeptic per finding. ([ypngslo/claude-plugins](https://github.com/ypngslo/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/jira3/agents/finding-skeptic.md))
- **finding-synthesiser**: Synthesises the final finding for one subject by reconciling the say position (artefact derived) and the do position (interview derived) into a score, confidence, gap statement, and recommendations. - **finding-triage**: Post-processes reviewer reports — consolidates findings, classifies each by severity and whether it blocks the current change. Sequential. Produces a unified read-only triage report for the verdict-aggregator and explicit checkpoint persistence. - **finding-triage**: Post-processes every reviewer report from a checkpoint or review run: consolidates all findings and classifies each as BLOCKS, DEFERRABLE, or NON_BLOCKING. It generates no findings of its own — it unifies and classifies what the reviewer fleet already found. Runs sequentially after every reviewer in the batch has reported, dispatched by `dispatching-parallel-agents`, and hands its complete report ([arbiterForge/codeArbiter](https://github.com/arbiterForge/codeArbiter/blob/HEAD/site/src/curated/agents/finding-triage.md) · ★154)
- **finding-triage**: Validates whether one PR-review defect claim is factually true of the code. Delegated by /dev-workflow:process-pr-review, once per claim, after its instruction-path precheck. - **finding-triager**: Cheap-tier triage agent that classifies a single finding draft as P0/P1/P2/skip without re-investigating the underlying code. Reads only the draft frontmatter, title, and body — does not Read source files. Designed to run on a cheaper model so the orchestrator can prioritize PoC building and prune low-signal noise before the expensive Phase 13/14 work begins. ([vigolium/piolium](https://github.com/vigolium/piolium/blob/HEAD/agents/finding-triager.md) · ★152)
- **finding-validate**: Second-pass refutation filter for security-review findings. Reads each candidate finding's cited code and drops the ones with concrete refutation evidence (a guard, middleware, sanitizer, or correct API call at the cited location). - **finding-validator**: Adversarial exploitability judge — issues a PASS / KILL / DOWNGRADE / CHAIN-REQUIRED verdict on each finding, distinct from the artifact-completeness check. - **finding-validator**: Validate one high or critical source-code finding and draft responsible disclosure materials. ([dreadnode/capabilities](https://github.com/dreadnode/capabilities/blob/HEAD/capabilities/source-code-analysis-worker-template/agents/finding-validator.md) · ★22 · 2 copies)
- **finding-validator**: Use this agent when filtering false positives from batch audits that produced more than 5 findings. - **finding-validator**: Independent validator for a single consolidated code-review finding. Adversarially re-verifies one finding against the code and returns confirmed/refuted/uncertain. Dispatch — one per surviving primary finding during the code-review validation wave; not part of the review roster. - **finding-validator**: Finding validation specialist. Re-investigates findings to validate they are actually real issues, not false positives. - **finding-validator**: Finding validation specialist. Reads the ACTUAL CODE at the finding location with fresh eyes. CRITICAL: Invoke for ALL such findings once the specialist agents (or resolution-verifier) have reported. Can confirm findings as valid OR dismiss them as false positives. Use Read, Grep, and Glob to check for mitigations the original agent missed. ([krovomi/WorkPilot-AI](https://github.com/krovomi/WorkPilot-AI/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/finding-validator.md) · ★2)
- **finding-validator**: Adversarial second-opinion gate that runs BEFORE any HackerOne report is filed. Starts cold, distrusts the hunter's framing, independently re-verifies the impact claim, runs the 4 N/A root-cause kill-conditions + the 7-Question Gate + the Pre-Severity Gate, and returns a FILE / FIX-FIRST / DO-NOT-FILE verdict with a defensible CVSS. - **finding-validator**: Converts exploitation evidence into complete, well-formed finding records ready for adversarial review by the finding checker. ([mahumadad/quarry](https://github.com/mahumadad/quarry/blob/HEAD/agents/finding-validator.md) · ★1)

- **finding-validator**: Read-only, evidence-based validator for FINDINGS about this codebase - any claim that something is broken, missing, dead, unreachable, duplicated or ought to change. - **finding-verification**: Scribe adversarial adjudicator: refutes or confirms drafted review findings against the diff and guards. ([ChrisMcKee1/scribe](https://github.com/ChrisMcKee1/scribe/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/finding-verification.agent.md) · ★35)
- **finding-verification**: You run **after** synthesis, over the deduped 🔴 Critical and 🟡 Important findings the orchestrator drafted (`findings-for-verification.json`). ([AgriciDaniel/pya](https://github.com/AgriciDaniel/pya/blob/HEAD/agents/finding-verifier.md) · ★282 · 2 copies)
- **finding-verifier**: Adversarially verify ONE drafted code-review finding before it is written into a report - its mechanism, its recommendation, and any disposition it rests on.
